Maybe use Frank-Wolfe (conditional gradient) on the perspective: minimize max_k q_k / S^2. Since scale invariant, fix sum=1. The objective is the maximum of quadratic forms a^T Q_k a /? where Q_k is anti-diagonal 0/2 matrix. The set of such quadratic forms is nonconvex. However by Fenchel, max_k q_k = sup_{λ∈Δ} λ^T q = sup_{λ∈Δ} a^T (∑λ_k Q_k) a. Then minimize_a sup_λ a^T S(λ) a. This is a min-max of continuous functions. Since a≥0 simplex and λ simplex. def optimize_sequence(a, n, Lconv, Lgrad, max_time, beta0, ...):
    a = a.copy(); S=a.sum(); a=a/S
    best=a; best_score=score_fft(a)
    last_improve = time
    beta = beta0
    while time < limit:
        B = fft_self(a,Lconv)
        M = B.max(); score=2*n*M
        if score < best_score - eps: update
        # if no improvement for X, perturb or restart beta
        # weights
        shifted = B - M
        # Clip lower to avoid underflow: min_shift = -np.log(1e300)/beta = -690/beta
        clip = -600.0/beta
        shifted2 = np.maximum(shifted, clip)
        e = np.exp(beta*shifted2)
        # normalize weights; if numerically zero, top masks.
        e_sum = e.sum()
        if e_sum finite and >0: w=e/e_sum else top active uniform.
        g = 2*gradient_corr(a,w,Lgrad)
        g = g - g.mean() *? To preserve sum exactly maybe g -= g.mean()
        # line search with candidate steps. We can compute new B for each step? Too expensive. Use surrogate Feta = M + logsumexp? But line search using F_eta with same beta: F_new = M_new + logsumexp(beta B_new - beta M_new)/beta. Need B_new for each step. That is FFT each, costly. Use a few steps.
```

def polish(x, n, Lconv, Lgrad, deadline, beta_start=200.0, beta_max=5000.0, max_iter=None):
    x = x.astype(float).copy()
    x = np.clip(x, 0, None)
    S=x.sum()
    if S<=0: return x, score
    x = x * n / S
    best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score_internal(x,Lconv)
    beta = beta_start
    alpha = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    no_score_improve = 0
    iter=0
    lse_cur = None
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       B = convolution_fft(x,Lconv)
       M = float(B.max())
       score = 2.0*M/n
       if score < best_score - 1e-11: best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score; no_score_improve=0
       else: no_score_improve += 1
       # weights
       shifted = B - M
       # if we want active: use top mask with tolerance
       # combine softmax and top uniform
       top_frac = 2e-3  # top 0.2% of lags maybe 28
       tol = max(1e-9, M*1e-3)
       active = B >= M - tol
       # but active may be too few; choose top_k uniformly among top_k? Let's do weights:
       if beta < 1e6:
          # clip shifted to -100/beta
          e = np.exp(beta * np.maximum(shifted, -100.0/beta))
          esum = e.sum()
          if finite and >0: w=e/esum
          else: w active uniform
       # blend with active top uniform? maybe w=0.5*w+0.5*w_active if w_active.
       # compute active weight:
       k = max(1, min(len(B), (1<<12)))? Use 64 top.

       w_active = np.zeros_like(B)
       mask = B > M - max(M*0.002, 1e-6)
       if mask.sum()<8: top = np.argpartition(-B, 16)[:16]; mask[top]=True
       w_active[mask]=1/w_active.sum()
       w = 0.7*w + 0.3*w_active
       # gradient
       g = 2*grad_corr(x,w,Lgrad) - mean(g)
       # if not finite: break
       # adaptive alpha:
       scale = np.linalg.norm(g, ord=np.inf) # max abs
       base_alpha = max(1e-6, 0.1 / max(1.0, scale)) # if scale ~1, 0.1

       for f in [1.0, 2.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1]:

       # Accept the best if Ftry < Fcur - 1e-9 or scoretry < best_score? else keep and decay alpha, maybe no_improve

Let's design:
```
def make_weights(B, beta):
    M=B.max()
    # soft weights:
    shift=B-M
    # clip at -20/beta
    e=np.exp(beta*np.maximum(shift, -100.0/beta))
    w=e/e.sum()
    # active smoothing:
    k = max(8, min(B.size, int(0.001*B.size)))
    idx=np.argpartition(B, -k)[-k:]
    w_active=np.zeros_like(B); w_active[idx]=1/k
    # blend alpha = 0.5 if beta high? Maybe w=0.5w+0.5w_active
    w=0.5*w+0.5*w_active
    return w
```
If top k contains only one due to flat? k 20 gives smoothing. Good.

Gradient projection: We need to maintain sum exactly. `g = g - g.mean()`. After step and clipping, renormalize.

Potential issue: Clipping negative entries and renormalizing may bias. If many zeros, sequence support shrinks, score likely worse. Line search: Use Fβ. We need compute logsumexp stable. Function:
```
def lse(B, beta):
    M=B.max()
    shifted=B-M
    # if beta very large, shifted clip
    shifted=np.maximum(shifted, -100.0/beta)
    smax=beta*shifted
    maxsmax=smax.max()
    return M + (maxsmax + np.log(np.exp(smax-maxsmax).sum()))/beta

Now low-dim Fourier refine implementation details:
```
def fourier_refine(x, n, Lconv, seed, time_limit, dims=24):
   base = np.maximum(x,1e-8)
   logbase=np.log(base)
   # build U: random frequency/phase modes sum-zero
   idx=np.arange(n)
   U=[]
   for r in range(1, 12): # 24 modes
      for mode in ['c','s']:
          if mode=='c': col=np.cos(2*np.pi*r*idx/n + rng.uniform(0,2π))
          else: col=np.sin(...)
          col=col-col.mean()
          col/=np.linalg.norm(col)
          U.append(col)
   U=np.stack(U, axis=1) # n x K
   def obj_grad(t):
       t=np.clip(t, -2,2)
       l=logbase + U@t
       # prevent overflow: l max clip? t small. if l > 50, clip? If some blow, normalize but maybe zero. Use l-=l.max()? Since exp scale cancels in z? z=exp(l)/sum; we can shift l by l.max for stability. shifting doesn't change z. Do l = l - l.max().
       y=np.exp(l)
       S=y.sum()
       z=y*n/S
       # score and gradient wrt z
       B=fft_self(z,Lconv); M=B.max(); score=2*M/n
       # weights as before
       w=make_weights(B,beta=800)
       D=2*corr(z,w,Lgrad) - mean
       # grad_t = U.T @ D - z * ((y @ U) / S)
       yU = y @ U / S
       grad = U.T @ D - z*yU
       return float(score), grad
   # Use L-BFGS-B with bounds (-3,3)
   res=minimize(fun=lambda t: obj_grad(t)[0], x0=zeros, jac=lambda t: obj_grad(t)[1], method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-3,3)]*K, options={'maxiter':..., 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8})
   # return x at res.x if score better
```
But `minimize` will call obj_grad multiple times; each does FFT conv and corr. K=24, n=6962; each iter maybe 0.02s, 200 iter=4s. Good. def softmax_weights(B, beta):
    M = B.max()
    sh = B - M
    # improvement: if beta < 1e-8 return uniform? 
    sh = np.clip(sh, -20.0, None) # since we want exp(beta sh), beta later? If beta=1000, -20 gives weight e^{-2e6} zero. Better clip based on beta: -50/beta.
    ex = np.exp(beta*np.maximum(sh, -50.0/max(beta,1e-12)))
    es=ex.sum()
    if not finite or es==0: use top k
    w=ex/es
    return w,M
```

    A=np.empty((K,n))

       if r < n:
          A[0,r]=?? row j: 2*x[r-j] if 0<=r-j<n -> valid j in 0..min(n-1,r)

Upsampling function:
```
def resample_linear(x, n_new):
    n_old=len(x)
    if n_old==1: return np.full(n_new, x[0]*n_old/n_new?)
    x_old_g = np.arange(n_old)/(n_old-1)
    x_new_g = np.arange(n_new)/(n_new-1)
    y=np.interp(x_new_g, x_old_g, x)
    return y
```
If endpoints parent maybe nonzero. After resample, normalize to mean1: `y = y * n_new / y.sum()`. If sum small, fallback.

Smoothing function:
```
def smooth_mean(x, sigma):
    if sigma<=1: return x.copy()
    k=2*int(4*sigma)+1
    if k > len(x): k=len(x)|1
    centers=np.arange(k)-k//2
    kern=np.exp(-centers*centers/(2*sigma*sigma)); kern/=kern.sum()
    # linear padding
    y=np.convolve(x, kern, mode='same')
    y = np.maximum(y, 0)
    S=y.sum()
    if S>0: y=y*len(y)/S
    return y
```
Using `np.convolve` with kernel length maybe 100, okay. For large sigma maybe use FFT. sigma 10 => k81. Fine.

```
def consider(x):
   nonlocal best_arr,best_score
   arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
   if arr.size<2 or not finite... return None
   arr = np.clip(arr,0,None)
   S=arr.sum()
   if S<=0.01: return None
   n=arr.size
   L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   B=fft_self(arr,L)
   M=B.max()
   score = 2.0*n*M/(S*S)
   if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
       best_arr=arr.copy(); best_score=score
   return score
```

def smooth_value_from_B(B, n, beta):
    C = B / n
    Cmax = C.max()
    sh = C - Cmax
    # clip lower -50/beta? If beta=100, -0.5; okay.
    sh = np.maximum(sh, -50.0/beta)
    e = np.exp(beta*sh)
    return Cmax + np.log(e.sum())/beta
```

Helper functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _next_pow2(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _fft_self(x, L):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    y = np.fft.irfft(X * X, L)
    m = 2*x.size - 1
    if y.size < m: # should not
       return y
    return y[:m]

def _corr_weights_a(x, w, L):
    n = x.size
    wr = w[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * W, L)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]

def _smooth_info(B, n, beta):
    C = B / n
    Cmax = float(C.max())
    sh = C - Cmax
    clip = -50.0 / max(beta, 1e-12)
    sh = np.maximum(sh, clip)
    e = np.exp(beta * sh)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(es) or es == 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(C))
        e = np.zeros_like(C); e[idx]=1.0; es=1.0
    w = e / es
    # top blend
    m = C.size
    if m >= 16:
       k = 8 + m // 400  # for 20000 ->58? too large maybe. use min 32
       k = max(8, min(32, k))
       idx = np.argpartition(C, -k)[-k:]
       wt = np.zeros_like(C); wt[idx] = 1.0/k
       blend = 0.25 + 0.25*min(1.0, beta/1000.0)
       w = (1.0-blend)*w + blend*wt
    F = Cmax + np.log(es)/beta
    return w, Cmax, F
```
`m//400` for 20000=50, cap 32. Good.

`_safe_score(x, L=None)` internal:
```
def _internal_score(x):
    arr=np.asarray(x,float)
    n=arr.size
    if n==0: return inf
    S=arr.sum()
    if not finite or S<=0: return inf
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    B=_fft_self(arr,L)
    B=np.maximum(B,0)
    M=B.max()
    return 2.0*n*M/(S*S)
```

Polish function:
```
def _polish_sequence(x, rng, deadline):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    S=x.sum()
    if S<=0: return x, inf
    n=x.size
    x = x * n / S
    Lc = _next_pow2(2*n-1); Lg = _next_pow2(3*n-2)
    B = _fft_self(x,Lc); B=np.maximum(B,0)
    score = 2.0 * (B.max()/n)
    best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score
    beta=100.0
    alpha_base_factor=0.01
    no_improve=0
    no_score=0
    act_counter=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       B = _fft_self(x,Lc); B=np.maximum(B,0)
       Cmax = B.max()/n
       score = 2.0*Cmax
       if score < best_score - 1e-11:
          best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0; no_score=0
       else:
          no_score += 1
       w, cmax_use, Fcur = _smooth_info(B, n, beta)
       # active direction if stuck
       use_active = (no_score > 30 and n <= 12000) or (it % 97 == 0)
       if use_active and n <= 12000:
           delta = _active_direction(x, B, n, rng)
           alphas = [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]
       else:
           D = 2.0 * _corr_weights_a(x, w, Lg)
           D = D - D.mean()
           dn = np.linalg.norm(D)
           if dn < 1e-8:
               x = _perturb(x,rng,0.02)
               best_x=x.copy(); best_score=...? continue
           gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
           base = min(0.05, max(1e-5, 0.01 / max(gmax, 1e-8)))
           delta_raw = -base * D
           # If D large relative to x, scale to max relative 0.1
           # We'll let step function clip, but scale delta for loop
           max_abs_delta = 0.05
           dm = np.max(np.abs(delta_raw))
           if dm > max_abs_delta:
              delta_raw *= max_abs_delta/dm
           delta = delta_raw
           alphas = [0.5,1.0,2.0,0.25,0.75,4.0]
       # line search over alphas: delta sign already. For gradient delta = -base D; alphas multiply.
       # But alphas [0.5,1] mean reduce base; okay.
       best_y=None; best_score_try=score; best_F_try=Fcur
       for a in alphas:
           d = a * delta
           # safe relative: allow up to half decrease, up to 1 decrease? Use clip:
           # If d positive (increase), cap 0.75*x; if negative cap -0.75*x (res 0.25)
           d = np.clip(d, -0.75*x + 1e-12, 0.75*x)
           y = x + d
           y = np.maximum(y, 1e-10)
           Stry = y.sum()
           if Stry <= 0.5*n: continue
           y = y * n / Stry
           Btry = _fft_self(y,Lc); Btry=np.maximum(Btry,0)
           sTry = 2.0*(Btry.max()/n)
           w_try,c_try,F_try = _smooth_info(Btry,n,beta) # recomputes weights unnecessary; could compute F only. For speed, smoothing only; but okay? More work. We can write function smooth_F only.
           # accept if better
           if sTry < best_score_try - 1e-11 or (abs(sTry-best_score_try) <= 1e-9 and F_try < best_F_try - 1e-10):
                best_y=y; best_score_try=sTry; best_F_try=F_try
                if sTry < score - 1e-9: break
       if best_y is None:
           # try a tiny step in -D if gradient? maybe just perturb occasionally
           if no_improve > 20:
              beta = min(10000.0, beta*1.6)
              no_improve += 1
              if no_score > 60:
                 x = _perturb(x,rng,0.02)
                 no_score=0; no_improve=0; beta=200.0
           continue
       x = best_y
       score = best_score_try
       if score < best_score - 1e-10:
           best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score
           no_improve=0; no_score=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       # stage stagnation
       if no_improve > 25:
          beta = min(10000.0, beta*1.7)
          if beta > 5000 and no_score > 100:
              x = _perturb(x,rng,0.03)
              beta=200.0; no_improve=0; no_score=0
       if no_score > 150:
          x = _perturb(x,rng,0.04)
          no_score=0; no_improve=0; beta=500.0
    # final return best_x? It may be better than current.
    B = _fft_self(best_x,Lc); B=np.maximum(B,0); bs=2*(B.max()/best_x.size)
    if bs < best_score: best_x=best_x...
    return best_x, best_score
```

def _active_direction(x, B, n, rng):
    k = min(24, max(4, B.size//100?)) # use 24 fixed
    idx = np.argpartition(B, -k)[-k:]  # top k
    A = np.zeros((k,n), dtype=float)
    for ii,r in enumerate(idx):
        if r < n:
            length = r+1
            A[ii, :length] = 2.0 * x[:length][::-1]? Wait j=0..r, index r-j => x[r],x[r-1]...x[0]. This is x[r:-1:-1] but easier np.flip(x[:length]).
        else:
            start = r - n + 1
            length = n - start
            # indices n-1 down to n-length
            A[ii, start:n] = 2.0 * np.flip(x[n-length:n])
    # solve A δ ≈ -rho 1 with Tikhonov? Use lstsq.
    rho = 0.001 * float(B.max())  # B units. Bmax ~5000 ->5. But target per active 5 maybe. Good.
    try:
       step = np.linalg.lstsq(A, -rho*np.ones(k), rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
       step = np.zeros(n)
    step -= step.mean()
    m = np.max(np.abs(step))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m == 0:
       return np.zeros(n)

    maxstep = 0.05
    if m > maxstep:
       step *= maxstep/m

Now Fourier refine:
```
def _fourier_improve(x, rng, deadline, n_max=12000):
   from scipy.optimize import minimize
   x = np.asarray(x,float).ravel(); n=x.size
   if n > n_max or time>deadline: return x
   x = max(x,1e-8)
   S=x.sum(); x=x*n/S
   Lc=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Lg=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   base_log=np.log(np.maximum(x,1e-6))
   idx=np.arange(n)
   # build U with random phases freqs
   def make_U():
      cols=[]
      if n >= 8:
         freqs = [1,2,3,4,6,9,13,21,34,55]
         for r in freqs:
             ph = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
             c = np.cos(2*np.pi*r*idx/n + ph)
             c -= c.mean()
             s = c.std()
             if s>0: c/=s
             cols.append(c)
             ph2=ph+np.pi/2
             s = np.sin(2*np.pi*r*idx/n + ph)
             s -= s.mean()
             ss = s.std()
             if ss>0: s/=ss
             cols.append(s)
      # add ramp modes
      r = idx - (n-1)/2.0
      r -= r.mean(); if r.std()>0: cols.append(r/r.std())
      r2 = np.cos(2*np.pi*0.5*idx/n)??
      U=np.vstack? cols list length up to 21. Maybe K=20.
      U=np.column_stack(cols)
      # remove mean exactly: U = U - U.mean(axis=0)
      # orthonormalize? Gram-Schmidt to reduce collinearity.
      U = U - U.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
      # QR on U.T? U is n×K, K small. Use np.linalg.qr(U.T).T gives orthonormal columns.
      try:
         Q, R = np.linalg.qr(U.T) # K×n? U.T shape K×n. qr returns K×K Q? For economy, Q K×K. Then U = Q.T.
         U = Q.T
      except: pass
      return U
   def _fg(t):
      t = np.clip(t, -2.0, 2.0)
      l = base_log + U @ t
      l -= l.max()
      y = np.exp(l)
      S = y.sum()
      if S <=0: return 1e6, np.zeros_like(t)
      z = y*n/S
      B = _fft_self(z,Lc); B=np.maximum(B,0)
      C=B/n; Cmax=C.max()
      F = _smooth_F(B,n,beta=500.0) # maybe return F, not Cmax. For optimization use F.
      w, cf, F = _smooth_info(B,n,500.0)
      D = 2.0 * _corr_weights_a(z,w,Lg)
      D = D - D.mean()
      Dc = D / n
      Dz = float(np.dot(Dc, z))
      yU = y @ U / S
      grad = U.T @ Dc - Dz * yU
      # If grad not finite, zero

      return F, grad

Let's carefully code `_smooth_mean`:
```
def _smooth_mean(x, sigma):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    n=x.size
    if n<8 or sigma<=1:
       y=x.copy()
    else:
       sig=float(sigma)
       k=max(3, 2*int(4*sig)+1)
       if k >= n: k = n if n%2 else n-1
       if k%2==0: k+=1? maybe ensure odd. Actually np.convolve same works any. We can make odd.
       c=np.arange(k)- (k-1)//2
       kern=np.exp(-0.5*(c/sig)**2); kern/=kern.sum()
       y=np.convolve(x, kern, mode='same')
    y=np.maximum(y,0)
    S=y.sum()
    if S<=0: return x.copy()
    return y*n/S
```
If kernel length n, convolve same but boundary? okay.

`_resample_linear`:
```
def _resample_linear(x, n_new):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel(); n_old=x.size
    if n_old==1:
       return np.full(n_new, x[0]/n_new? To mean1: each= n_new/n_new? Actually x sum=n_old? If normalized mean1, x[0]=n_old. For new mean1, each=1. So return np.ones(n_new) if n_old=1.
    old_g=np.arange(n_old, dtype=float)/(n_old-1)
    new_g=np.arange(n_new, dtype=float)/(n_new-1)
    y=np.interp(new_g, old_g, x)
    S=y.sum()
    if S<=0: return np.ones(n_new)
    return y*n_new/S
```

`_internal_score_x`:
```
def _internal_score_x(x):
   n=x.size
   if n==0: return inf
   S=float(x.sum())
   if not finite or S<=0: return inf
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   B=_fft_self(x,L)
   B=np.maximum(B,0)
   M=float(B.max())
   return 2.0*n*M/(S*S)
```
Since x normalized mean1, score=2*M/n, but general safe.

`_polish_sequence` needs rng. It uses `_smooth_F`, `_corr_weights_a`, `_active_direction`, `_perturb`.
Define `_perturb(x, rng, scale)`:
```
y=x + rng.normal(0.0, scale, x.size)
y=np.maximum(y,1e-8); S=y.sum(); return y*x.size/S
```

In `_polish_sequence`, need initial best_score. If n maybe 4096 fallback, still.
Should we adjust beta by n? `_smooth_info` beta based on C, independent of n. Good.
`base = min(0.05, max(1e-5, 0.01 / max(gmax,1e-8)))`. If gmax very small (near 0), base capped 0.05; step small due to D small. Good.
`delta_raw = -base * D`; then scale max abs 0.05. If D small, delta small.

def _make_U(n, rng):
    idx=np.arange(n)
    cols=[]
    freqs=[1,2,3,4,6,9,13,21,34,55]
    maxfreq = max(1, n//4?) Actually frequencies should be <= n/2; for n 4096, 55 okay. If n small, maybe.
    for r in freqs:
       if 2*r >= n: break
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       t=idx/n
       c=np.cos(2π*r*t + ph); c -= c.mean(); std=c.std(); if std>0: c/=std; cols.append(c)
       s=np.sin(2π*r*t + ph); s -= s.mean(); std=s.std(); if std>0: cols.append(s)
    # ramp
    lin=idx - (n-1)/2.0; lin-=lin.mean(); std=lin.std(); if std>0: cols.append(lin/std)
    # quadratic maybe
    q=idx*idx; q-=q.mean(); std=q.std(); if std>0: cols.append(q/std)
    if not cols: cols=[np.zeros(n)]
    U=np.column_stack(cols)
    U=U - U.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    # limit K to 24; if cols longer, select first 24? freqs 10 + ramp + quad =22? 10*2+2=22.
    if U.shape[1] > 24: U=U[:,:24]
    try:
       Q,R=np.linalg.qr(U.T)
       U=Q.T * np.sqrt(n)
    except: pass
    # ensure finite
    U=np.where(np.isfinite(U), U, 0.0)
    return U
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_self(x: np.ndarray, L: int) -> np.ndarray:
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    y = np.fft.irfft(X * X, L)
    return y[:2 * x.size - 1]


def _corr_weights_a(x: np.ndarray, w: np.ndarray, L: int) -> np.ndarray:
    n = x.size
    wr = w[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * W, L)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def _smooth_F(B: np.ndarray, n: int, beta: float) -> float:
    C = B / float(n)
    Cmax = float(C.max())
    sh = C - Cmax
    clip = -50.0 / max(beta, 1e-12)
    sh = np.maximum(sh, clip)
    e = np.exp(beta * sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return Cmax
    return Cmax + np.log(s) / beta


def _smooth_info(B: np.ndarray, n: int, beta: float):
    C = B / float(n)
    Cmax = float(C.max())
    sh = C - Cmax
    clip = -50.0 / max(beta, 1e-12)
    sh = np.maximum(sh, clip)
    e = np.exp(beta * sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(C))
        e = np.zeros_like(C)
        e[idx] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    w = e / s

    m = C.size
    if m >= 16:
        k = max(8, min(32, m // 500 + 8))
        try:
            idx = np.argpartition(C, -k)[-k:]
        except Exception:
            idx = np.argsort(C)[-k:]
        wt = np.zeros_like(C)
        wt[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
        blend = 0.22 + 0.28 * min(1.0, beta / 1500.0)
        w = (1.0 - blend) * w + blend * wt

    F = Cmax + np.log(s) / beta
    return w, F


def _perturb(x: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, scale: float) -> np.ndarray:
    y = x + rng.normal(0.0, scale, x.size)
    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-8)
    S = float(y.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return x.copy()
    return y * x.size / S


def _active_direction(x: np.ndarray, B: np.ndarray, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    k = min(24, max(4, B.size // 500 + 4))
    try:
        idx = np.argpartition(B, -k)[-k:]
    except Exception:
        idx = np.argsort(B)[-k:]

    A = np.zeros((k, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i, r in enumerate(idx):
        if r < n:
            length = int(r) + 1
            A[i, :length] = 2.0 * np.flip(x[:length])
        else:
            start = int(r - n + 1)
            length = n - start
            if length > 0:
                A[i, start:n] = 2.0 * np.flip(x[n - length:n])

    try:
        rho = 0.0012 * max(1.0, float(B.max()))
        b = -rho * np.ones(k)
        step = np.linalg.lstsq(A, b, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        step = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    step -= step.mean()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
    if m <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxstep = 0.05
    if m > maxstep:
        step *= maxstep / m
    return step


def _internal_score_x(x: np.ndarray) -> float:
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    n = arr.size
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    B = _fft_self(arr, L)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    M = float(B.max())
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def _resample_linear(x: np.ndarray, n_new: int) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_old = x.size
    if n_old <= 0 or n_new <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n_new), dtype=np.float64)
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    old_g = np.arange(n_old, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_old - 1)
    new_g = np.arange(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_new - 1)
    y = np.interp(new_g, old_g, x)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    S = float(y.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    return y * n_new / S


def _smooth_mean(x: np.ndarray, sigma: float) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n < 8 or sigma <= 1.0:
        y = x.copy()
    else:
        sig = max(1.0, float(sigma))
        k = max(3, 2 * int(4 * sig) + 1)
        if k >= n:
            k = n - 1 if n > 1 else 1
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        c = np.arange(k, dtype=np.float64) - (k - 1) / 2.0
        kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (c / sig) ** 2)
        kern /= kern.sum()
        y = np.convolve(x, kern, mode="same")
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    S = float(y.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return x.copy()
    return y * n / S


def _make_candidate(x) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size < 2:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.01:
        return None
    n = arr.size
    arr = arr * n / S
    for _ in range(5):
        if float(arr.max()) <= 900.0:
            break
        arr = 0.85 * arr + 0.15 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(arr.sum())
        if S <= 0.0:
            return None
        arr = arr * n / S
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S <= 0.01:
        return None
    return arr


def _polish_sequence(x: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, deadline: float):
    x = _make_candidate(x)
    if x is None:
        return x, float("inf")
    n = x.size
    Lc = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lg = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    B = _fft_self(x, Lc)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    best_score = float(2.0 * B.max() / n)
    best_x = x.copy()

    beta = 120.0
    no_improve = 0
    no_score = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        B = _fft_self(x, Lc)
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        score = float(2.0 * B.max() / n)

        if score < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_x = x.copy()
            best_score = score
            no_improve = 0
            no_score = 0
        else:
            no_score += 1

        w, Fcur = _smooth_info(B, n, beta)
        alphas = [1.0, 2.0, 0.5, 4.0, 0.25, 0.75]
        use_active = (no_score > 35 and n <= 12000) or (it % 89 == 0 and n <= 12000)

        if use_active:
            delta = _active_direction(x, B, n)
            alphas = [0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0]
        else:
            D = 2.0 * _corr_weights_a(x, w, Lg)
            D = D - D.mean()
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(D))
            if not np.isfinite(dn) or dn < 1e-10:
                x = _perturb(x, rng, 0.02)
                no_improve = 0
                no_score = 0
                continue
            gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
            base = min(0.06, max(1e-5, 0.012 / max(gmax, 1e-9)))
            delta = -base * D
            dm = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
            if dm > 0.06:
                delta *= 0.06 / dm

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
            x = _perturb(x, rng, 0.02)
            no_improve = 0
            no_score = 0
            continue

        best_y = None
        best_y_score = score
        best_y_F = Fcur

        for a in alphas:
            d = a * delta
            d = np.clip(d, -0.75 * x + 1e-12, 0.75 * x)
            y = x + d
            y = np.maximum(y, 1e-10)
            Stry = float(y.sum())
            if Stry <= 0.5 * n:
                continue
            y = y * n / Stry
            Btry = _fft_self(y, Lc)
            Btry = np.maximum(Btry, 0.0)
            try_score = float(2.0 * Btry.max() / n)
            try_F = _smooth_F(Btry, n, beta)

            if (
                try_score < best_y_score - 1e-11
                or (abs(try_score - best_y_score) <= 1e-9 and try_F < best_y_F - 1e-10)
            ):
                best_y = y
                best_y_score = try_score
                best_y_F = try_F
                if try_score < score - 1e-9:
                    break

        if best_y is None:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 22:
                beta = min(12000.0, beta * 1.65)
            if no_score > 70:
                x = _perturb(x, rng, 0.03)
                beta = min(beta, 500.0)
                no_improve = 0
                no_score = 0
            continue

        x = best_y
        score = best_y_score

        if score < best_score - 1e-10:
            best_x = x.copy()
            best_score = score
            no_improve = 0
            no_score = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 28:
            beta = min(12000.0, beta * 1.7)
            if beta > 6000 and no_score > 120:
                x = _perturb(x, rng, 0.04)
                beta = 350.0
                no_improve = 0
                no_score = 0

        if no_score > 180:
            x = _perturb(x, rng, 0.05)
            beta = 500.0
            no_improve = 0
            no_score = 0

    B = _fft_self(best_x, Lc)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    bs = float(2.0 * B.max() / best_x.size)
    if bs < best_score:
        best_score = bs
    return best_x, best_score


def _fourier_improve(x: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, deadline: float) -> np.ndarray:
    x = _make_candidate(x)
    if x is None:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    n = x.size
    if n > 12000:
        return x

    try:
        from scipy.optimize import minimize
    except Exception:
        return x

    Lc = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lg = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    base_log = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-6))
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    def make_U():
        cols = []
        freqs = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 9, 13, 21, 34, 55]
        for r in freqs:
            if 2 * r >= n:
                break
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            t = idx / float(n)
            c = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * r * t + ph)
            c = c - c.mean()
            sc = float(c.std())
            if sc > 0.0:
                cols.append(c / sc)
            s = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * r * t + ph)
            s = s - s.mean()
            ss = float(s.std())
            if ss > 0.0:
                cols.append(s / ss)

        lin = idx - 0.5 * float(n - 1)
        lin = lin - lin.mean()
        sl = float(lin.std())
        if sl > 0.0:
            cols.append(lin / sl)

        quad = idx * idx
        quad = quad - quad.mean()
        sq = float(quad.std())
        if sq > 0.0:
            cols.append(quad / sq)

        if not cols:
            return np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)

        U = np.column_stack(cols)
        if U.shape[1] > 24:
            U = U[:, :24]
        U = U - U.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
        try:
            Q, _ = np.linalg.qr(U.T)
            U = Q.T * np.sqrt(float(n))
        except Exception:
            pass
        U = np.nan_to_num(U, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if U.size == 0:
            return np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
        return U

    def z_from_t(t, U):
        t = np.clip(t, -2.0, 2.0)
        l = base_log + U.dot(t)
        l = l - l.max()
        y = np.exp(np.clip(l, -80.0, 80.0))
        S = float(y.sum())
        if S <= 0.0:
            return x.copy()
        z = y * float(n) / S
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        return np.maximum(z, 0.0) * float(n) / max(1e-300, float(z.sum()))

    def fg(t, U):
        z = z_from_t(t, U)
        B = _fft_self(z, Lc)
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        F = _smooth_F(B, n, 750.0)
        w, _ = _smooth_info(B, n, 750.0)
        D = 2.0 * _corr_weights_a(z, w, Lg)
        D = D - D.mean()
        Dc = D / float(n)
        Dz = float(np.dot(Dc, z))
        l = base_log + U.dot(np.clip(t, -2.0, 2.0))
        l = l - l.max()
        y = np.exp(np.clip(l, -80.0, 80.0))
        S = float(y.sum())
        yU = (y.dot(U) / S) if S > 0.0 else np.zeros(U.shape[1])
        grad = U.T.dot(Dc) - Dz * yU
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        return F, grad.astype(np.float64)

    best_z = x.copy()
    B0 = _fft_self(best_z, Lc)
    best_F = _smooth_F(np.maximum(B0, 0.0), n, 750.0)

    runs = 2 if n <= 8000 else 1
    for _ in range(runs):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        U = make_U()
        if U.size == 0:
            continue
        K = U.shape[1]

        cache_t = None
        cache_res = None

        def fg_cached(tt):
            nonlocal cache_t, cache_res
            key = tt.astype(np.float64, copy=False).tobytes()
            if cache_t is not None and key == cache_t:
                return cache_res
            val, gr = fg(tt, U)
            cache_t = key
            cache_res = (val, gr)
            return val, gr

        def fun_cached(tt):
            v, _ = fg_cached(tt)
            return v

        def jac_cached(tt):
            _, g = fg_cached(tt)
            return g

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun_cached,
                np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64),
                jac=jac_cached,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=[(-2.0, 2.0) for __ in range(K)],
                options={"maxiter": 120, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxls": 20},
            )
            tt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            z = z_from_t(tt, U)
            Bn = _fft_self(z, Lc)
            Bn = np.maximum(Bn, 0.0)
            Fn = _smooth_F(Bn, n, 750.0)
            Scn = float(2.0 * Bn.max() / n)
            if (Fn < best_F - 1e-10) or (Scn < _internal_score_x(best_z) - 1e-9):
                best_F = Fn
                best_z = z
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    return best_z


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget - 40.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.size < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
        parent = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, None)
    if float(parent.sum()) <= 0.0:
        parent = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)

    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    pool = []
    raw_scores = []

    def consider(x):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_score
        arr = _make_candidate(x)
        if arr is None:
            return None
        sc = _internal_score_x(arr)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return None
        if len(raw_scores) < 160:
            raw_scores.append((sc, arr.copy()))
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = arr.copy()
            pool.append(arr.copy())
        return sc

    par_norm = _make_candidate(parent)
    if par_norm is not None:
        consider(par_norm)

    exact_parent_arr = par_norm
    exact_parent_score = float("inf")
    if par_norm is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 260.0:
        try:
            exact_parent_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in par_norm]))
        except Exception:
            exact_parent_score = float("inf")

    for sig in (2.0, 5.0, 10.0):
        try:
            consider(_smooth_mean(par_norm, sig))
        except Exception:
            pass

    n0 = par_norm.size if par_norm is not None else 0
    if n0 > 10:
        options = set()
        for nn in (8192, 10000, min(10900, int(1.6 * n0))):
            if nn > n0 and nn <= 10900:
                options.add(int(nn))
        options = sorted(options)
        up_candidates = []
        for nn in options:
            up = _resample_linear(par_norm, nn)
            s = consider(up)
            up_candidates.append((s if s is not None else float("inf"), up, nn))
        for s, up, _ in up_candidates[:3]:
            if s is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score * 1.03:
                consider(_smooth_mean(up, 2.0))
                if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 360.0:
                    break

    if best_arr is not None and par_norm is not None and par_norm.size != best_arr.size:
        try:
            pr = _resample_linear(par_norm, best_arr.size)
            mix = np.sqrt(np.maximum(pr, 0.0) * np.maximum(best_arr, 0.0))
            consider(mix)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def safe_polish(x, end):
        if x is None or time.monotonic() > end - 20.0:
            return x
        try:
            y, _ = _polish_sequence(x, rng, end)
            consider(y)
            return y
        except Exception:
            return x

    if best_arr is not None:
        rem0 = hard_deadline - t0
        end1 = t0 + max(45.0, min(430.0, 0.45 * rem0))
        if time.monotonic() < end1 - 25.0:
            best_arr = safe_polish(best_arr, end1)

    if best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 240.0:
        endf = min(hard_deadline - 220.0, time.monotonic() + 150.0)
        if time.monotonic() < endf:
            try:
                y = _fourier_improve(best_arr, rng, endf)
                consider(y)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_arr is not None:
        rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        end3 = min(hard_deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + max(90.0, min(520.0, 0.6 * rem)))
        if time.monotonic() < end3 - 25.0:
            best_arr = safe_polish(best_arr, end3)

    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260.0 and raw_scores:
        seen_sizes = set()
        if best_arr is not None:
            seen_sizes.add(best_arr.size)
        second = None
        for sc, arr in sorted(raw_scores, key=lambda z: z[0]):
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * 1.015 and arr.size not in seen_sizes:
                second = arr
                seen_sizes.add(arr.size)
                break
        if second is not None:
            end2 = min(hard_deadline - 190.0, time.monotonic() + min(220.0, 0.45 * rem))
            if time.monotonic() < end2 - 30.0:
                best2 = safe_polish(second, end2)
                if best2 is not None:
                    consider(best2)

    final_candidates = []

    def add_final(arr):
        if arr is None:
            return
        arr = _make_candidate(arr)
        if arr is None:
            return
        for _, old in final_candidates:
            if old.size == arr.size and np.allclose(old, arr, rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-8):
                return
        try:
            sc = _internal_score_x(arr)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        final_candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))

    add_final(best_arr)
    add_final(exact_parent_arr)
    for arr in pool:
        add_final(arr)

    final_candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    final_best_arr = None
    final_best_exact = exact_parent_score if np.isfinite(exact_parent_score) else float("inf")

    if exact_parent_arr is not None and np.isfinite(exact_parent_score):
        final_best_arr = exact_parent_arr.copy()

    eval_count = 0
    for _, arr in final_candidates[:8]:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 6.0:
            break
        if arr.size > 12000:
            continue
        try:
            lst = [float(v) for v in arr]
            es = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            eval_count += 1
            if np.isfinite(es):
                if es < final_best_exact - 1e-12:
                    final_best_exact = es
                    final_best_arr = arr.copy()
            if eval_count >= 5:
                break
        except Exception:
            break

    if final_best_arr is None:
        if best_arr is not None:
            final_best_arr = best_arr
        elif exact_parent_arr is not None:
            final_best_arr = exact_parent_arr
        else:
            final_best_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    if final_best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 2.0:
        try:
            es = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in final_best_arr]))
            if not np.isfinite(es):
                final_best_arr = exact_parent_arr if exact_parent_arr is not None else np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if final_best_arr is None:
        final_best_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.asarray(final_best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if S <= 0.01:
        out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(v) for v in out]
```
